```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a non-empty value is printed with a new record.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a non-empty value and a new record flag set to true.
      2. During printing, the print(...) method is reached and the new record branch is taken.
      3. The value is printed with the appropriate formatting.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable output and a single non-empty string value to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly reflects the printed value, including any necessary formatting for new records.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when an empty value is printed with a new record.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with an empty value and a new record flag set to true.
      2. During printing, the print(...) method is reached and the empty value branch is taken.
      3. The output is processed to handle the empty value correctly.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable output and an empty string value to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output reflects the handling of the empty value, ensuring it does not disrupt the record format.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null value is printed with a new record.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a null value and a new record flag set to true.
      2. During printing, the print(...) method is reached and the null value branch is taken.
      3. The output is processed to handle the null value according to the defined nullString.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable output and a null value to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly represents the null value according to the defined nullString behavior.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the quote mode is set to ALL and a numeric value is printed.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a numeric value and the quote mode set to ALL.
      2. During printing, the printAndQuote(...) method is reached and the numeric value is processed.
      3. The numeric value is encapsulated in quotes as per the quote mode policy.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable output and a numeric value to print, ensuring the quote mode is set to ALL.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output includes the numeric value encapsulated in quotes, adhering to the ALL quote mode policy.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the quote mode is set to NONE and a string value is printed.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a string value and the quote mode set to NONE.
      2. During printing, the printAndQuote(...) method is reached and the string value is processed.
      3. The string value is printed without any encapsulation.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable output and a string value to print, ensuring the quote mode is set to NONE.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output reflects the string value printed without any quotes, as dictated by the NONE quote mode policy.
```

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test quote handling for values containing special characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that includes special characters (e.g., a comma or newline).
      2. During printing, print(...) is reached and the special character handling branch is taken.
      3. The output is formatted correctly with the necessary escaping or quoting.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes a string with special characters such as commas and newlines.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes or quotes the special characters according to the CSV format rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the quote character is set and a value contains it.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that contains the quote character.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for handling the quote character is executed.
      3. The output is formatted with the necessary quoting around the value.
    input_focus: |
      Include a value in the input that contains the quote character defined in the CSV format.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output includes the value wrapped in quotes, with any internal quotes escaped as necessary.

  - design_name: |
      Test trimming behavior when surrounding spaces are ignored.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CharSequence trim(final CharSequence charSequence)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(final boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true) to configure the format.
      2. During formatting, trim(...) is reached and the trimming behavior is applied.
      3. The formatted output should have leading and trailing spaces removed.
    input_focus: |
      Set up a CSVFormat instance and provide a value with leading and trailing spaces.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output does not include any leading or trailing spaces in the formatted values.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the trailing delimiter is configured.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withTrailingDelimiter()
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withTrailingDelimiter() to enable trailing delimiters.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the trailing delimiter handling branch is executed.
      3. The output should include a trailing delimiter after the last value.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with trailing delimiters enabled and provide a set of values to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output ends with the specified delimiter, confirming that the trailing delimiter is applied.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(final String recordSeparator)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out) 
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withRecordSeparator("\n") to set a custom record separator.
      2. During printing, the custom separator is applied when records are output.
      3. The output should reflect the custom record separator.
    input_focus: |
      Set up a CSVPrinter with a custom record separator and provide multiple records to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output uses the custom record separator between records as specified.
```

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintWithNullValueHandlesIOException() throws IOException
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test quote handling for values containing special characters.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, CSVFormat.DEFAULT);
          printer.printRecord("Value, with, commas", "Value with\nnew line");
          String expectedOutput = "\"Value, with, commas\",\"Value with\nnew line\"\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the quote character is set and a value contains it.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithQuoteCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithQuoteCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value with \"quote\"");
          String expectedOutput = "\"Value with \"\"quote\"\"\"\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test trimming behavior when surrounding spaces are ignored.
    test_name: testTrimWithSurroundingSpacesIgnored
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testTrimWithSurroundingSpacesIgnored() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("   Value with spaces   ");
          String expectedOutput = "\"Value with spaces\"\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the trailing delimiter is configured.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter(true);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value1", "Value2");
          String expectedOutput = "Value1,Value2,\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withRecordSeparator("\n");
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Record1");
          printer.printRecord("Record2");
          String expectedOutput = "Record1\nRecord2\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test printing behavior with various configurations.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a set of values to print.
      2. The print(...) method is invoked for each value in the array.
      3. The branch for handling the first value (newRecord) is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of mixed values including nulls, strings, and numbers to observe how they are printed.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly reflects the formatting rules applied for the first record and subsequent values.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when escape character is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that contains characters needing escaping.
      2. The printAndEscape(...) method is reached when the escape character is set.
      3. The escaping logic is executed for the characters in the value.
    input_focus: |
      Use a string that includes the escape character and other special characters to trigger the escape logic.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to the defined escape rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when quote character is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that requires quoting.
      2. The printAndQuote(...) method is invoked to handle the quoting logic.
      3. The branch for handling the quoting of the value is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a string that contains characters that necessitate quoting, such as commas or quotes.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly encapsulates the value with the quote character as needed.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when comment marker is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isCommentMarkerSet()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final char commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() with a valid comment marker.
      2. The isCommentMarkerSet() method is invoked to check if the marker is set.
      3. The branch for confirming the presence of the comment marker is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Set a comment marker and verify that it is recognized by the format.
    observable_behavior: |
      Ensure that the format correctly identifies the comment marker and that it can be used in parsing.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null string is defined.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isNullStringSet()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withNullString(final String nullString)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withNullString() to set a custom null string.
      2. The isNullStringSet() method is invoked to check if the null string is defined.
      3. The branch confirming the presence of the null string is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Set a specific string to represent null values in the format.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the format recognizes the custom null string and applies it correctly during parsing and printing.
```

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test printing behavior with mixed values including nulls, strings, and numbers.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithMixedValues
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithMixedValues() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, CSVFormat.DEFAULT);
          printer.printRecord("Value1", null, 42, "Value4");
          String expectedOutput = "Value1,NULL,42,Value4\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
  
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when escape character is set.
    test_name: testPrintWithEscapeCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintWithEscapeCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.print("Value with escape \\ character");
          String expectedOutput = "Value with escape \\\\ character\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when quote character is set.
    test_name: testPrintWithQuoteCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintWithQuoteCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.print("Value with, comma");
          String expectedOutput = "\"Value with, comma\"\n";
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when comment marker is set.
    test_name: testWithCommentMarker
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithCommentMarker() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withCommentMarker('#');
          assertTrue(format.isCommentMarkerSet());
      }

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when null string is defined.
    test_name: testWithNullStringSet

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a character is less than or equal to the comment marker.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include a string starting with a character less than or equal to the comment marker.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for handling this character is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a string value that starts with a character that is less than or equal to the comment marker.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly handles the character according to the defined behavior for comment markers.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a character is a line break or delimiter during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include a string containing line break or delimiter characters.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for handling these characters is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Include a string value that contains line break or delimiter characters.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly escapes or quotes the line break or delimiter characters as per the CSV format rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when trimming is applied to values during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include strings with leading and trailing spaces.
      2. During printing, print(...) is reached and the trimming behavior is evaluated.
    input_focus: |
      Provide strings that have leading and trailing spaces to observe the trimming behavior.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the printed output does not include leading or trailing spaces, reflecting the trimming behavior.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the trailing delimiter is set to true.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values and ensure the trailing delimiter is set to true.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the branch for adding the trailing delimiter is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Set the CSVFormat to allow a trailing delimiter and provide any values for printing.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output includes the trailing delimiter after the last value printed.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values and set a custom record separator.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the custom record separator is applied.
    input_focus: |
      Configure the CSVFormat with a custom record separator and provide values for printing.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output uses the custom record separator instead of the default line separator.
```

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a character is less than or equal to the comment marker.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithCharacterLessThanOrEqualToCommentMarker
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithCharacterLessThanOrEqualToCommentMarker() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withCommentMarker('#');
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("#This is a comment");
          String expectedOutput = "\"#This is a comment\""; // Expect the output to be quoted
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a character is a line break or delimiter during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLineBreakOrDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLineBreakOrDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withDelimiter(',');
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value1,Value\n2,Value3");
          String expectedOutput = "\"Value1,Value\\n2\",Value3"; // Expect the line break to be escaped
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when trimming is applied to values during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("   Value1   ", "   Value2   ");
          String expectedOutput = "Value1,Value2"; // Expect the output to have no leading/trailing spaces
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the trailing delimiter is set to true.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter(true);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Record1", "Record2");
          String expectedOutput = "Record1,Record2,"; // Expect the output to include a trailing delimiter
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withRecordSeparator(";\n");
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Record1");
          printer.printRecord("Record2");
          String expectedOutput = "Record1;\nRecord2;\n"; // Expect the output to use the custom record separator
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());

```yaml
      Test behavior when escape character is set.
    method_context:

        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a CSVFormat that has an escape character set.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached when special characters are encountered.
      3. The escape character handling branch is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value containing special characters (e.g., delimiter, escape character) and ensure the CSVFormat is configured with an escape character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to the defined escape character.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when quote character is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a CSVFormat that has a quote character set.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached when values require quoting.
      3. The quote character handling branch is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that contains characters needing encapsulation (e.g., the quote character itself) and ensure the CSVFormat is configured with a quote character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly encapsulates the value with the quote character as needed.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when comment marker is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isCommentMarkerSet()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final char commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() with a valid comment marker.
      2. The isCommentMarkerSet() method is reached to check if comments are enabled.
      3. The branch for handling comments is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Set a comment marker (e.g., '#') and ensure it is recognized in the CSVFormat.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the CSVFormat correctly identifies the comment marker and handles comments appropriately.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null string is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isNullStringSet()

        public CSVFormat withNullString(final String nullString)

      Set a specific null string (e.g., "\\N") in the CSVFormat.
```

```yaml
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null values are passed to the header array.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private String[] toStringArray(final Object[] values)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(final String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with a null array.
      2. The toStringArray(...) method is invoked, leading to the null check.
      3. The branch handling the null input is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a null value to the withHeader() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method handles the null input gracefully, possibly returning a null or empty header array.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when an enum is passed to define headers.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(final Class<? extends Enum<?>> headerEnum)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with a valid enum class.
      2. The method retrieves enum constants and enters the loop to process them.
      3. The branch for handling enum values is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a valid enum class with defined constants.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the resulting header array contains the correct string representations of the enum constants.

  - design_name: |
      Test trimming behavior on leading and trailing spaces.
    method_context:
```

    test_name: testPrintRecordWithEscapedCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithEscapedCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withDelimiter(',');
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value1", "Value\n2", "Value,3");
          String expectedOutput = "Value1,\"Value\n2\",Value,3"; // Expect the output to escape the newline
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString().trim());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote(null);
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value1,Value2");
          String expectedOutput = "Value1,Value2"; // Expect no quotes applied
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString().trim());
      }

    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLineBreak() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format);
          printer.printRecord("Value1", "Value\n2"); // Include a line break

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when ignoring surrounding spaces is enabled.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CharSequence trim(CharSequence charSequence)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces)
```
